[2025-08-30T12:20:21.632559Z] Dr.K.M.RAJENDIRAN: @all This is to bring to the attention of all staff members the following guidelines regarding attendance, leave, and late arrival through the HR App: \n  \n Attendance Processing \n  \n Attendance in the HR App will be processed twice every month: \n  \n Attendance From 1st to 15th, any time after 15th of the month \n  \n Attendance From 16th to end of the Month any time at the end of the month \n  \n Application for Leave / Permission / On Duty (OD) \n  \n All leave, permission, or OD requests must be applied immediately without any delay. \n  \n Once the attendance is processed, no application or correction will be entertained. \n  \n Failure to apply on time will result in no remedy from the Management. \n  \n  \n Accuracy of Date and Time \n  \n While applying for leave or permission, staff must ensure correct selection of date and time. \n  \n Any wrong entry will not be reversed or corrected later. \n  \n Late Arrival Policy \n  \n Only 2 hours of permission is allowed for late arrival. \n  \n No other late arrival option is available. \n  \n Any late arrival beyond the permitted 2 hours will be treated as Loss of Pay (LOP) automatically. \n  \n All staff are requested to strictly adhere to the above instructions. Thank you

[2026-03-09T12:13:10.678287Z] Dr.K.M.RAJENDIRAN: @all Dear Staff, if any colleague is missing from the Main Office Group, please add them to ensure everyone is included.
[2026-03-09T12:14:10.625583Z] Dr.K.M.RAJENDIRAN: @all CIRCULAR \n To: All Senior Learners \n This is a reminder to all Senior Learners to avoid any unnecessary confusion regarding the attendance policy. \n All staff members are requested to strictly adhere to the institutional attendance policy. The HR App attendance system has been configured with the following rules for late attendance: \n  \n If a staff member marks attendance after the permitted grace period, the HR App will automatically apply one Short Time Off, provided the balance for the respective month is available. \n  \n If the Short Time Off balance has already been utilized, the HR App will automatically apply Half-Day Casual Leave. \n  \n If Casual Leave balance is not available, the HR App will automatically apply Half-Day Loss of Pay (LOP). \n  \n All staff members are therefore advised to remain vigilant regarding their attendance and leave balances to avoid any unintended leave deductions or Loss of Pay. \n The attendance policy is system-driven, and the institution will not entertain requests for manual corrections arising from negligence or lack of attention. \n Your cooperation in maintaining punctuality and institutional discipline is highly appreciated.                          \n By Order of the Management
[2026-03-10T10:54:22.134351Z] Dr.K.M.RAJENDIRAN: @all Dear Dental Staff, \n For staff members working six days a week, the work schedule has been revised to 9:05 AM to 3:30 PM. \n Previously, the schedule was 9:01 AM to 3:30 PM. Hence, kindly follow the revised schedule with immediate effect.  Actual official Time in the morning is 9 am, grace time 5 minutes. so staff can punch till 9 hr 4 minutes 59 seconds. If arrived after 9.05 am the HR app will treat as late. \n If you report after the permitted time, please apply for Short Time Off or the appropriate option in the HR App, as applicable. \n Your cooperation in adhering to the revised schedule and attendance policy is appreciated.
[2026-03-14T04:19:59.861727Z] Dr.K.M.RAJENDIRAN: @all LONG HOLIDAY ATTENDANCE AND LOSS OF PAY (LOP) POLICY \n  \n 1. Purpose \n  \n The purpose of this policy is to ensure the availability of faculty members immediately before and after \n institution-declared long holidays and to maintain smooth academic and administrative operations. \n  \n 2. Scope \n  \n This policy applies to all faculty members and staff of the Institution. \n  \n 3. Policy Statement \n  \n When the Institution declares a long holiday period, all faculty members are required to be present for duty on: \n  \n The last  working day preceding the holiday, and \n The first  working day mmediately following the holiday. \n  \n 4. Attendance Requirement \n  \n Faculty members must ensure their physical presence and official attendance on both of the above-mentioned days. \n  \n Failure to report for duty on either one or both days will result in the entire holiday period being treated as Loss of Pay (LOP). \n  \n 5. Exceptions \n  \n The above rule will not apply under the following circumstances: \n  \n When  the faculty member is assigned University-related duties, including  but not  limited to: \n External  Examiner duties University examination work \n Any  other official work assigned by the University When the faculty member is assigned official duties by the Management or the  Head of the Institution. \n  \n In such cases, the absence will be considered Official Duty (OD) and no Loss of Pay will be applied. \n  \n 6. Approval and Documentation \n  \n Faculty members assigned to official duties must submit valid supporting documents or approval from the concerned authority to the institution for records. \n  \n 7. Non-Compliance \n  \n If a faculty member is absent on the required working days without valid approval or official assignment, the entireholiday duration will be treated as Loss of Pay (LOP) in accordance with institutional policy. \n  \n 8. Authority \n  \n The Management and the Head of the Institution reserve the right to interpret, implement, and enforce this policy.                                                                                                                                                                                      ON DUTY (OD) AND CASUAL LEAVE (CL)POLICY \n  \n 1. Purpose \n  \n This policy is intended to regulate the granting of On Duty (OD) for faculty members attending University-related official work and to ensure proper attendance before and after such duties. \n  \n 2. Scope \n  \n This policy applies to all faculty members of the Institution.  \n  \n 3. On Duty (OD) Eligibility \n  \n Faculty members may be granted On Duty (OD) for officially assigned University-related work, including but not limited to: \n  \n University Examination Invigilation Duty \n Paper Evaluation / Valuation \n University Inspection Duties \n Any other official work \n      assigned by the University \n  \n 4. Attendance Requirement \n  \n Faculty members who are granted On Duty (OD) must: \n  \n Be physically present in the Institution on the working day immediately before the OD, and \n Be physically present in the Institution on the working day immediately after the OD. \n  \n 5. Casual Leave Restriction \n  \n Faculty members shall not combine Casual Leave with On Duty (OD) for the purpose \n of extending the leave period. \n  \n 6. Loss of Pay (LOP) Provision \n  \n If a faculty member: \n  \n Fails to report for duty on the working day before the OD, or Fails to report for duty on \n      the working day after the OD, the OD will not be approved, and the entire period will be treated as      Loss of Pay (LOP). \n  \n 7. Documentation Requirement \n  \n Faculty members must submit valid supporting documents or official communication \n from the University confirming the assigned duty. \n  \n 8. Implementation Authority \n  \n The Head of the Institution and HR Department are responsible for implementing and \n monitoring this policy.                                                                                                                                                                           LEAVE AND ON DUTY RESTRICTION DURING EXIT MONTH \n  \n  \n 1. Policy Statement \n  \n Faculty members who have submitted their resignation and are serving the notice period (exit month) will not be permitted to avail On Duty (OD) or Casual Leave (CL) during the exit period. \n  \n 2. Attendance Requirement \n  \n Faculty members must physically report for duty on all working days during the exit month \n to ensure proper handover of academic, administrative, and institutional responsibilities. \n  \n 3. Leave Restriction \n  \n During the exit month: \n  \n On Duty (OD) will not be entertained. \n Casual Leave (CL) will not be permitted. \n  \n  \n 4. Loss of Pay (LOP) Provision \n  \n If a faculty member fails to report for duty during the exit month without prior approval, the absence will be treated as Loss of Pay (LOP) as per institutional rules. \n  \n 5. Exceptional Cases \n  \n Any exception to this policy may be granted only with the approval of the Head of \n the Institution and Management.
